> 2026-11-01 新审计说明：下列旧叙述为历史记录；当前结论以本轮 strict evidence 及文末全量重审记录为准。

# wpnnoise 历史审核台账与 trunk 覆盖边界（Issue #147 F2）

本轮仅将当前 glossary authority 重绑定至 Tranquillity 拼写兼容别名后的摘要；旧权威字段复原后逐域重现原 inventory 摘要，baseline、生产 dump、卡片正文和结论保持原样。真实 trunk 内容增量另列审查，不由此重绑定推导通过。

## 历史边界

严格 JSONL 块只覆盖历史提交 `7b56bccf9ce06646b65acf056b1445ad2999512d` 的 65 个 identity；其中 keep、route、变体数和 proposal 都是该快照的事实，不能当成当前 trunk 的全量通过结论。历史卡片保持冻结，以保留原审核和候选协议验证；本次不把新增生产键强塞入旧 schema 或放宽历史门禁。

当前术语绑定为 `61a07d128c39a38b568eea73b3fef9b15cf76ac85900f458820bfcd22df09dc0`；哈希更新不表示重新独立审查旧译文。

## 当前 trunk 的增量事实与审查证据

以下增量按 `cb4d884e18` 源码/英文和冻结中文 `c55cf7f7a5` 核对。新增键及新增变体在历史卡片中存在明确覆盖缺口，不能由旧卡片的 keep 推导通过；B2 database 独立审查覆盖这些新增内容，delta/delta2 确认要求的修订已闭合。此处列出事实、对应证据和缺口，不生成未经审查的结论。

- 独立证据：`issue147-b2-review-database.md`（SHA-256 `da4d0ebee23b34732ab96dd091d117246eca631545c6ade3d263f9e200e9cb68`，覆盖统计及“已核对、无问题的要点”）；`issue147-b2-review-delta.md`（SHA-256 `bacff995ecb7392730b4c956ba7befc1e42b8b3d3210f1b08b51b34665a57da3`）；`issue147-b2-review-delta2.md`（SHA-256 `00973e387a86ec5aa9cee449010846dc0c05e4e4b1c68f1615c36fced42137c7`）。
- EN source SHA-256: `a303a726f66d936c8e689b1bdab547c582aa8446154beae101873f1f7927e792`
- ZH source SHA-256: `a9205eb4d1ee315c561e3aaf1ea3782076bd20b6e7dc048e07a1ab0db9718b0d`

<!-- BEGIN ISSUE147 TRUNK COVERAGE -->
| 当前键 | 历史键 | 分类 | 当前 EN 变体 | 当前 ZH 变体 |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|
| `fire dragon occultist scales farewell` | `—` | new-key | 2 | 2 |
| `fire dragon occultist scales greeting` | `—` | new-key | 3 | 3 |
| `ice dragon arcanist scales farewell` | `—` | new-key | 2 | 2 |
| `ice dragon arcanist scales greeting` | `—` | new-key | 3 | 3 |
| `_real_song_no_tension_` | `_real_song_no_tension_` | new-variant | 22 | 22 |
<!-- END ISSUE147 TRUNK COVERAGE -->

4 个龙鳞 greeting/farewell 来自上游 6c2c9e6e9b，键数 65→69；旧台账没有这 4 张卡。上游 ce5df4a3ae 为 `_real_song_no_tension_` 新增第 22 个 EN 变体：`@The_weapon@ sings, "May the bird of paradise fly up your nose..."`。B2 明确审核龙鳞 4 键及这句歌词；历史卡片仍只绑定原 21 个 EN 变体及其当时 proposal。

brightly asks, \"How about a duet?\"","@The_weapon@ wonders, \"Am I just an instrument of [doom|death|destruction]?\"","@The_weapon@ yells, \"No battle, no fun!\"","@The_weapon@ shouts, \"Make war, not love!\"","@The_weapon@ cries out, \"You certainly can't call me dull!\"","@The_weapon@ tells you the story of its previous owner's [demise|death|misfortune].","@The_weapon@ brightly announces, [silent|uncommunicative|taciturn|quiet|boring]?","@The_weapon@ grouses, \"Silence can be so depressing.\"","@The_weapon@ grouses, \"Silence always makes me sulk.\"","@The_weapon@ compliments you on act.\""],"current_chinese":["@The_weapon@叫道：“和我一起唱歌！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“一，二，三……”","@The_weapon@高谈阔论着音乐的知识。","@The_weapon@[指出|随口提了一句]，不存在唱歌[斧|棒|长柄武器|杖]。","@The_weapon@开始了一段关于杀人音乐的冗长独白。","@The_weapon@在为自己的歌声得意自鸣。","@The_weapon@询问你对@_musical_topic_@的看法。","@The_weapon@沉思地自言自语，感慨人们为什么再也造不出这么好的剑了。","@The_weapon@试图即兴创作韵文，但惨遭失败。","@The_weapon@想为你唱你最喜欢的歌曲。","@The_weapon@要求你押“@_rhyme_word_@”的韵。","@The_weapon@在抱怨[双刃剑|三刃剑|短剑]。","@The_weapon@在嘲弄短剑。","@The_weapon@推敲着“对唱”和“对决”的联系。","@The_weapon@哀号道：“我还年轻，不能生锈！”","@The_weapon@评论着你的音乐品味。","@The_weapon@抱怨你对音乐缺乏热情。","@The_weapon@试图拉你一起唱卡农。","@The_weapon@告诉你它对创作一部宏伟的歌剧的计划。","@The_weapon@抱怨道：“我想有一个听众总比没有强……”","@The_weapon@沉吟道：“如果你唱了歌，但没有人听到，那你真的唱过歌吗？”","@The_weapon@欢快地问：“来个二重唱怎么样？”","@The_weapon@想弄明白：“我只是一个制造[厄运|死亡|毁灭]的工具吗？”","@The_weapon@喊道：“无战不欢！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“要作战，不要做爱！”","@The_weapon@喊道：“你当然不能说我迟钝！”","@The_weapon@向你诉说其前主人[败亡|死亡|不幸]的故事。","@The_weapon@欢快地说：“我有很多话要告诉你！”","@The_weapon@说：“你也可以说些什么，你知道的。”","@The_weapon@抱怨道：“你总是这么[沉默|孤僻|缄默|安静|无聊]吗？”","@The_weapon@发着牢骚：“沉默是如此令人沮丧。”","@The_weapon@发着牢骚：“沉默总是让我生闷气。”","@The_weapon@赞美你对武器的品味。","@The_weapon@试图教你一些绕口令。","@The_weapon@说：“至少有一天你会死去，逃脱这沉闷的生活！”","@The_weapon@说：“我不熟悉那首歌，但如果你哼几个小节，我可以试一下。”","@The_weapon@吹嘘道：“你知道，我的音域有八个八度。”","@The_weapon@惆怅地叹息道：“我希望我的音乐能像我的杀人事迹一样受到关注……”","@The_weapon@冷笑道：“@player_name@，我听过你的嗓子，还是让我独唱吧。”"],"proposed_translation":["@The_weapon@叫道：“和我一起唱歌！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“一，二，三……”","@The_weapon@高谈阔论着音乐的知识。","@The_weapon@[指出|随口提了一句]，不存在唱歌[斧|棒|长柄武器|杖]。","@The_weapon@开始了一段关于杀人音乐的冗长独白。","@The_weapon@在为自己的歌声得意自鸣。","@The_weapon@询问你对@_musical_topic_@的看法。","@The_weapon@沉思地自言自语，感慨人们为什么再也造不出这么好的剑了。","@The_weapon@试图即兴创作韵文，但惨遭失败。","@The_weapon@想为你唱你最喜欢的歌曲。","@The_weapon@要求你押“@_rhyme_word_@”的韵。","@The_weapon@在抱怨[双刃剑|三刃剑|短剑]。","@The_weapon@在嘲弄短剑。","@The_weapon@推敲着“对唱”和“对决”的联系。","@The_weapon@哀号道：“我还年轻，不能生锈！”","@The_weapon@评论着你的音乐品味。","@The_weapon@抱怨你对音乐缺乏热情。","@The_weapon@试图拉你一起唱卡农。","@The_weapon@告诉你它对创作一部宏伟的歌剧的计划。","@The_weapon@抱怨道：“我想有一个听众总比没有强……”","@The_weapon@沉吟道：“如果你唱了歌，但没有人听到 I'm the Singing Sword!\"","@The_weapon@ chants, \"I am golden and pointed, and with blood well anointed.\"","@The_weapon@ dictates a lengthy tome entitled 'The Well-Tempered Blade'.","@The_weapon@ says, \"Whips just don't cut it for me, don't you agree?\"","@The_weapon@ chants, \"Rather stab than club, rather slice than rub!\"","@The_weapon@ states, \"Music critics are all talk and no body armour.\"","@The_weapon@ demands, \"I must not be sheathed without first drawing blood!\"","@The_weapon@ [explains all about|gives a lengthy lecture on] @_musical_topic_@.","@The_weapon@ curses its smith, \"I was supposed to be the Stinging Sword!\"","@The_weapon@ yells, \"Ploughshares to swords!\"","@The_weapon@ asks, \"What song would you like to be played for your funeral?\"","@The_weapon@ announces, \"I dedicate these songs to @player_name@!\"","@The_weapon@ declares, \"I sing the tale of @player_name@, the warrior!\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"Hey! Are you even listening?\"","@The_weapon@ longs back for the days in the arena.","@The_weapon@ amuses itself with detailed descriptions of past executions.","@The_weapon@ wishes everyone in the dungeon [an unpleasant|a sudden] [demise|death].","@The_weapon@ fumes, \"Being battered, bent and broken sure is better than this boredom!\"","@The_weapon@ shouts, \"I love putting a song in people's hearts. And in their kidneys.\"","@The_weapon@ grouses, \"Could you please kill something? I'm itching all over.\"","@The_weapon@ laments, \"What are you waiting for? I am doing all the work.\"","@The_weapon@ explains, \"Killing is an art, not a pastime.\"","@The_weapon@ goes, \"Jessica and Sigmund, sitting in a tree...\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"I want to meet the serpentine blade!\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"Take me to Pandemonium! Now!!!\"","@The_weapon@ recounts funny stories of hydra head multiplication.","@The_weapon@ says, \"Sometimes people call me a magnificent bastard.\"","@The_weapon@ snarls, \"Dear diary, yet another boring day with @player_name@...\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"In my experience, @player_genus_plural@ are mediocre at best.\"","@The_weapon@ groans, \"Among all @player_genus_plural@, @player_name@ is the worst.\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"Unhand me, foul miscreant!\"","@The_weapon@ muses, \"Whom do I have to kill to get some service around here?\"","@The_weapon@ sings out, \"Sing loudly and carry a big sword!\"","@The_weapon@ ponders the difference in lifespan between man and sword.","@The_weapon@ says, \"All work and no play makes @player_name@ a dull boy!\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"I wish I'd pick another to be my killing [tool|fool].\"","@The_weapon@ hoarsely says, \"Need a drink... quick! Stab something.\"","@The_weapon@ says, \"You haven't happened to see my sheath laying around anywhere? I feel so exposed.\"","@The_weapon@ complains, \"Oh, are your palms wet and greasy. But I allow you to wield me for a while.\""],"current_chinese":["@The_weapon@说：“嗨！我是歌唱之剑！”","@The_weapon@吟唱道：“我是金质的，尖锐的，并用血祝过圣。”","@The_weapon@口述了一部名为《平均律刃曲集》的著作。","@The_weapon@说：“鞭子对我来说根本不顶用，你说呢？”","@The_weapon@吟唱道：“宁刺不棍击，宁切不擦！”","@The_weapon@陈述：“音乐评论家只会动嘴，身上连护甲都没有。”","@The_weapon@要求：“还没有让敌人流血，就不要把我收回剑鞘！”","@The_weapon@就@_musical_topic_@这个话题进行了[详细阐述|长篇大论]。","@The_weapon@诅咒着锻造它的铁匠：“我本应是那把刺叮剑！”","@The_weapon@喊道：“铸犁为剑！”","@The_weapon@问：“你想在葬礼上演奏哪首歌？”","@The_weapon@宣布：“我将这些歌献给@player_name@！”","@The_weapon@宣告：“我要歌颂勇士@player_name@的故事！”","@The_weapon@说：“嘿！你在听吗？”","@The_weapon@渴望回到在竞技场的日子。","@The_weapon@叙说着过去的行刑细节以自娱自乐。","@The_weapon@希望地牢中的每个人都有[一个不愉快的|一个突如其来的][败亡|死法]。","@The_weapon@怒气冲冲地说：“即使被打扁、被打弯、被打碎也绝对比现在百无聊赖要强！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“我喜欢把歌曲放在人们的心里。还有他们的肾脏里。”","@The_weapon@发着牢骚：“你能杀些什么吗？我浑身发痒。”","@The_weapon@悲叹道：“你在等什么？我在做所有工作。”","@The_weapon@解释道：“杀戮是一门艺术，而不是消遣。”","@The_weapon@唱道：“杰西卡和西格蒙德，坐在树上……”","@The_weapon@说：“我想见见蛇刃！”","@The_weapon@说：“把我带到万魔殿！马上！！！”","@The_weapon@讲述着多头蛇头部数量倍增的有趣故事。","@The_weapon@说：“有时人们说我是个了不起的混蛋。”","@The_weapon@咆哮道：“亲爱的日记，今天又是和@player_name@无聊的一天……”","@The_weapon@说：“根据我的经验，@player_genus_plural@充其量只算得上是平庸。”","@The_weapon@哼道：“在所有@player_genus_plural@之中，@player_name@是最差的。”","@The_weapon@说：“放开我，肮脏的恶棍！”","@The_weapon@沉吟道：“我必须杀掉谁才能在这里获得某些服务？”","@The_weapon@大声唱道：“高声歌唱，手持大剑！”","@The_weapon@在思考人和剑在寿命上的差异。","@The_weapon@说：“只工作不玩耍，@player_name@也会变傻！”","@The_weapon@说：“我希望我选择了另一个人作为杀戮[工具|傻瓜]。”","@The_weapon@沙哑地说：“需要喝一杯……快！要刺些什么东西。”","@The_weapon@说：“你有没有见到我的剑鞘放到了什么地方？我感觉自己太暴露了。”","@The_weapon@抱怨道：“哦，你的手又湿又油。但我允许你使用我一段时间。”"],"proposed_translation":["@The_weapon@说：“嗨！我是歌唱之剑！”","@The_weapon@吟唱道：“我是金质的，尖锐的，并用血祝过圣。”","@The_weapon@口述了一部名为《平均律刃曲集》的著作。","@The_weapon@说：“鞭子对我来说根本不顶用，你说呢？”","@The_weapon@吟唱道：“宁刺不棍击，宁切不擦！”","@The_weapon@陈述：“音乐评论家只会动嘴，身上连护甲都没有。”","@The_weapon@要求：“还没有让敌人流血，就不要把我收回剑鞘！”","@The_weapon@就@_musical_topic_@这个话题进行了[详细阐述|长篇大论]。","@The_weapon@诅咒着锻造它的铁匠：“我本应是那把刺叮剑！”","@The_weapon@喊道：“铸犁为剑！”","@The_weapon@问：“你想在葬礼上演奏哪首歌？”","@The_weapon@宣布：“我将这些歌献给@player_name@！”","@The_weapon@宣告：“我要歌颂勇士@pla But dauntlessly - I defy you all!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Too hard for you... because you're too stupid!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Bring it in there... as I command! Hahei!]\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a lilting soprano, \"Badly you guard... the sleeping gold; watch better o'er... the slumberer's bed!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Damp fills my nostrils: curse this sneezing!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"...for hear me, ye waves: thus I curse love!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Thank you, stupid! Your work has stood the test.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"The whole world will I win... all for my own!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"All you gods I'll grip... in my golden grasp!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"As I renounced love, all living things... shall love renounce!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Beware! Beware! For when once you men... serve my might...\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Beware the nocturnal host... when the Abyssal horde rises... from the silent depths... to the day's light!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"With wisdom the fool... is puffed up to bursting! Now may envy devour you!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Take heed, haughty god! If I sinned, I sinned only 'gainst myself!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"But you, immortal one, sin against... all that was, is and shall be!\"\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"It shall gladden... no happy man; its bright gleam... shall light on no one lucky!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"Many a time have I paid... giants their due.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"A sultry haze... hangs in the air; its dull weight... oppresses me.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"Heda! Heda! Hedo!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"...its glittering glow hides... runes of riches...\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"As you command, I will willingly lead you - steeply down: shall we journey to the Orb?\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"But for all the world... I will not surrender the Orb!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Then shall we swing down... through the sulphurous cleft?\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Oh! Oh! Terrible serpent, do not swallow me!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Slink off home! No knot holds you: go your way freely!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a piercing soprano, \"Woe! Woe is me!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a squeaky tenor, \"Oh, Oh! Ow! Ow! Ow!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a melting tenor, \"How soft the breeze... which breathes on us again!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Your power lends you... high-flying courage: grim and great... has grown your strength.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"For moon, stars and the radiant sun... may, too, do no other... than to serve you!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Take whatever shape you wish... with wonder strike me dumb!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a cavernous basso, \"Ah, her gaze... still gleams toward me; the stars of her eyes... still shine on me!\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Irredeemably... to dark destruction... its possession dooms you.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Hear me! Hear me! Hear me! All that is shall come to an end.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"A dark day... dawns for the gods.\"","@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Whatever was - I know; whatever is, whatever shall be - I also see.\""],"current_chinese":["@The_weapon@唱道：“随他去，顺其自然……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“今晚滑血多快乐，把滑血歌儿唱！”","@The_weapon@唱道：“这首歌将我温柔处死……”","@The_weapon@[唱道|吹着口哨]：“总是看着死亡中光明的一面……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“这将是我死去的那一天……”","@The_weapon@用刺耳的声音唱道：“@player_name@，与我共长眠吧！”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我渴望一位英雄！”","@The_weapon@悲哀地问：“@player_name@，今晚我可以出去杀人吗？”","@The_weapon@唱道：“……一点也不怕被残忍地杀死，许多许多残忍的方式……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我是个战士，而不是个爱人。”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我在地牢里刺了个人，就为了眼睁睁地看着他断气。”","@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“[嗨啊呀嗨啊！嗨啊呀嗨啊！|哇啦啦嗨啊！哇啦啦，哇啊啦嗨啊！|哇啦啦！啦啦哎呀！哎呀啦哎！|嗨啊！嗨啊！哈嘿！]”","@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“这黄金的沉睡，你们守得真糟……要更好地看护它沉睡的温床！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“潮湿填满了我的鼻子：该死的喷嚏！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“……听好了，波浪，我如此诅咒这爱！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“谢谢你，蠢货！你的活干得不错。”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“把整个世界……据为自己所有！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“用黄金之拳，我会攥住……你们所有的神！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“就像我把爱拒绝，一切的生灵……该把它弃绝！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神！留神！因为你们这些人一旦……臣服于我的力量……”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神这暗夜的大军……当深渊的大军从死寂之底升起、迎向日光！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“一点小聪明……就胀得要炸的蠢货！愿嫉妒将你吞噬！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“当心了，高高在上的神！我若有罪，这罪也只对我自己！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“但你这不朽者，所犯的罪行违背了……过去、现在和将来的一切！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“没有愉者……该为它欢愉，它明亮的光辉……不会照到任何幸运者身上！”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“付……巨人报酬，我可是常客。”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“闷热的水汽……悬在空中；我恼于……这沉闷压迫！”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“嘿哒！嘿哒！嘿哆！”","@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“财富的符文藏在……它的闪光之中……”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你的命令，我乐意带领你——我们深入地底：可要寻求宝珠？”","@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“但为全世界……我都不会交出宝珠！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“那我们就荡下去……穿过那硫磺裂隙？”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“哦唉！哦唉！可怕的巨蛇，别吞了我！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“溜回家吧！没绳子绑着你了，随便走吧！”","@The_weapon@用尖厉的女高音唱道：“悲啊！悲啊！”","@The_weapon@用尖细的男高音唱道：“哦！哦！嗷！嗷！嗷！”","@The_weapon@用柔和的男高音唱道：“多可爱的风……又为我们吹起！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“你的力量赋予你……凌云的勇气：你的力量……已变得可怖而强大。”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“因为明月星辰，和那光辉的太阳……它们也不敢做别的……只能去服务你！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你变成什么模样……只要能让我惊叹到说不出话！”","@The_weapon@用空洞的低音唱道：“唉呀，她的目光……仍闪烁着；她眼里的星……仍照耀着我！”","@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“拥有它，必将使你万劫不复地堕入黑暗的毁灭！”","@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“听着！听着！听着！所有现存的一切都将走向终结。”","@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“黑暗的一天……向众神降临。”","@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“过去的一切——我都知晓；现在和将来的一切——我也看得见。”"],"proposed_translation":["@The_weapon@唱道：“随他去，顺其自然……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“今晚滑血多快乐，把滑血歌儿唱！”","@The_weapon@唱道：“这首歌将我温柔处死……”","@The_weapon@[唱道|吹着口哨]：“总是看着死亡中光明的一面……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“这将是我死去的那一天……”","@The_weapon@用刺耳的声音唱道：“@player_name@，与我共长眠吧！”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我渴望一位英雄！”","@The_weapon@悲哀地问：“@player_name@，今晚我可以出去杀人吗？”","@The_weapon@唱道：“……一点也不怕被残忍地杀死，许多许多残忍的方式……”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我是个战士，而不是个爱人。”","@The_weapon@唱道：“我在地牢里刺了个人，就为了眼睁睁地看着他断气。”","@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“[嗨啊呀嗨啊！嗨啊呀嗨啊！|哇啦啦嗨啊！哇啦啦，哇啊啦嗨啊！|哇啦啦！啦啦哎呀！哎呀啦哎！|嗨啊！嗨啊！哈嘿！]”","@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“这黄金的沉睡，你们守得真糟……要更好地看护它沉睡的温床！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“潮湿填满了我的鼻子：该死的喷嚏！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“……听好了，波浪，我如此诅咒这爱！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“谢谢你，蠢货！你的活干得不错。”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“把整个世界……据为自己所有！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“用黄金之拳，我会攥住……你们所有的神！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“就像我把爱拒绝，一切的生灵……该把它弃绝！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神！留神！因为你们这些人一旦……臣服于我的力量……”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神这暗夜的大军……当深渊的大军从死寂之底升起、迎向日光！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“一点小聪明……就胀得要炸的蠢货！愿嫉妒将你吞噬！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“当心了，高高在上的神！我若有罪，这罪也只对我自己！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“但你这不朽者，所犯的罪行违背了……过去、现在和将来的一切！”","@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“没有愉者……该为它欢愉，它明亮的光辉……不会照到任何幸运者身上！”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“付……巨人报酬，我可是常客。”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“闷热的水汽……悬在空中；我恼于……这沉闷压迫！”","@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“嘿哒！嘿哒！嘿哆！”","@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“财富的符文藏在……它的闪光之中……”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你的命令，我乐意带领你——我们深入地底：可要寻求宝珠？”","@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“但为全世界……我都不会交出宝珠！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“那我们就荡下去……穿过那硫磺裂隙？”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“哦唉！哦唉！可怕的巨蛇，别吞了我！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“溜回家吧！没绳子绑着你了，随便走吧！”","@The_weapon@用尖厉的女高音唱道：“悲啊！悲啊！”","@The_weapon@用尖细的男高音唱道：“哦！哦！嗷！嗷！嗷！”","@The_weapon@用柔和的男高音唱道：“多可爱的风……又为我们吹起！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“你的力量赋予你……凌云的勇气：你的力量……已变得可怖而强大。”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“因为明月星辰，和那光辉的太阳……它们也不敢做别的……只能去服务你！”","@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你变成什么模样……只要能让我惊叹到说不出话！”","@The_weapon@用空洞的低音唱道：“唉呀，她的目光……仍闪烁着；她眼里的星……仍照耀着我 SHRINK!\""],"current_chinese":["@The_weapon@说：“啦啊啊啊！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我被锻造出来是为了毁坏世界！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我要唱那首终结世界的歌！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是笔！我是剑！我是至高的领主！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是你的惩戒者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“今天是审判日，判决死刑！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是创造者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“赏赐的是剑，收取的也是剑！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我受过血的洗礼！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“感受一下精神净化！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“所有人都崇拜我，我是钢铁的至尊！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“向我祈祷，而不是向骨肉之神祈祷！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“血液喷泉是为了向美丽的剑刃致敬！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是第五位天启骑士！时候到了！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我成了死神，[世界|诸神|勇士|英雄|军团|国家|百万人]的[毁灭者|污染者|肢解者]！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“复活在我，生命也在我！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“这就是你所知道的世界末日！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“如果我是上帝，每个人都得死！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“扩散出去！摧毁一切！尽情放纵！尽情享受！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是被赋予使命的钢铁！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是寻求宣泄的痛苦！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“让我们勒死那些@_MISCREANTS_@，勒死他们！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“杀！杀！！杀！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我闻到了血腥味！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“要屠杀，不要戏法！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“杀戮，仇恨，残害！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“谋杀！死亡！！杀戮！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“毁灭，终止，根除！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我在鲜血、骨头和脑浆中沐浴！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是战争使者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“让我们结束他们悲惨的一生！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“打倒他们！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“在我唱歌的时候死去！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“血已没膝，势头正盛！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“再来一曲！再来一曲！再来一曲！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“你在这种时候怎么还想着唱歌？！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“尖叫声！不要让他们停止尖叫！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“没有人能[活下来|逃脱|幸存|幸免]！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“不要放过任何人！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“到我这里来！到我这里去死吧！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“砍！砍！砍啊劈啊！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“刺击，捅、捅、捅！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“放弃所有希望！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“砍了他们！把他们全砍了！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“让我们进行劈砍！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“你别死在我面前@CAPS@ @player_name@！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“这是痛苦的颜色！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“对！对！对！这就是生活，宝贝，这就是生活！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“把它涂成红色！把它涂成红色！把它全部涂成红色红色红色红色！！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“[用力|快点]甩我，你这个[笨拙的|无能的|无用的|呆滞的|手臂软绵绵的|流着眼泪的|野蛮粗鄙的][傻子|胖子|弱者|竹节虫|平足者|傻瓜|白痴|树懒]！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“要么受苦，要么灭亡！随便你！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我打破了魔法！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“[@CAPS@ @_kind_of_scales_@音阶的|音阶不和谐的]杀戮！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“快点，@CAPS@ @player_genus@，杀杀杀！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“鲜血！鲜血！鲜血必须喷涌，鲜血必须涌出！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“鲜血！鲜血！每个敌人都将血流如注！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“脑浆！脑浆！劈开头骨，它就会渗出！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“脑浆！脑浆！杀戮很有趣，生命很廉价！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是佐特之剑！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@，“我是佐特符文和亮光！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我爱你们所有人！我送给你们的礼物便是末日决战！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我的爱是永恒的！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我兴起了风暴！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“让我们抚摸@_PETS_@，抚摸它们！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是一个[鲜血|舞蹈|死亡|荒芜|绝望|破坏|末日|血污|疯狂|音乐|痛楚|毁灭|杀戮|歌曲|钢铁|战争][天使|艺术家|化身|先驱者|乐器|诗人|先知|仆从|奴隶|领主|主宰]！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是一只美丽的动物！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“一切都将毁灭！一切！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“涅！涅！！涅！！！涅！！！涅！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是性感的光脚战神！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“无厘头，千年手和虾！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是死亡之铁！任何鲜血都无法让我退缩！”"],"proposed_translation":["@The_weapon@说：“啦啊啊啊！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我被锻造出来是为了毁坏世界！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我要唱那首终结世界的歌！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是笔！我是剑！我是至高的领主！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是你的惩戒者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“今天是审判日，判决死刑！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是创造者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“赏赐的是剑，收取的也是剑！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我受过血的洗礼！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“感受一下精神净化！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“所有人都崇拜我，我是钢铁的至尊！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“向我祈祷，而不是向骨肉之神祈祷！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“血液喷泉是为了向美丽的剑刃致敬！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是第五位天启骑士！时候到了！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我成了死神，[世界|诸神|勇士|英雄|军团|国家|百万人]的[毁灭者|污染者|肢解者]！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“复活在我，生命也在我！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“这就是你所知道的世界末日！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“如果我是上帝，每个人都得死！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“扩散出去！摧毁一切！尽情放纵！尽情享受！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是被赋予使命的钢铁！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我是寻求宣泄的痛苦！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“让我们勒死那些@_MISCREANTS_@，勒死他们！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“杀！杀！！杀！！！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我闻到了血腥味！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“要屠杀，不要戏法！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“杀戮，仇恨，残害！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“谋杀！死亡！！杀戮！！！”","@The_we Die!\"","@The_weapon@ shouts, \"You shall lie beneath the ground but I, I will glint in the sun!\"","@The_weapon@ shouts, \"Born from this womb, sliced by this blade!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"When this war is won, I will wage the next one.\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blade-deep in blood!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"I revel, I sever!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"All those pillows!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"No rest until they move no more!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"As long as they die, we live!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"The future is bloody!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"The bloody future is ours!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Dismember the living, dismember the dead!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"We will celebrate on a pile of carcasses!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"@player_name@, you will [dine|feast] on fine @_corpses_@ today!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Go for the jugular!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My instinct to kill is stronger than their will to live!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"I am covered in blood! It feels so good!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Aah! This piece of @_body_part_@ is stuck to me. Wipe it off!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My edge gets dull! My edge gets dull!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Don't stick me into that one, please.\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My victories add up!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blood as far as the eye can see! Blood for all eternity!\"","@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blood must spurt and blood must flow! Blood must gush from every foe!\""],"current_chinese":["@The_weapon@喊道：“我在此以鲜血祝圣这些殿堂！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“情绪失控？胡说八道！我的钢铁永远坚韧！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“砍了他们！立刻砍掉他们所有的小脑袋！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“把他们都杀了，让{{ if you.god() == \"No God\" then\nreturn \"@_godless_sorter_@\"; else return \"@player_god@\"; end }}来收拾他们！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“尖叫和哭泣是完美的伴奏。”","@The_weapon@叫道：“我要用你的肠道做钢琴弦！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“哦，钢铁割肉的@_glorious_@感觉。”","@The_weapon@叫道：“我确实喜欢搜肠刮肚。”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“在所有这些迷失的灵魂之中，我们至高无上！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“死。死。死！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“你会躺在地下，而我，我会在阳光下闪闪发光！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“从这个子宫诞生，被这把刀割开！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“当这场战争胜利后，我将发动下一场战争。”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“刀口舔血！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我狂欢，我斩断！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“那些不过是绣花枕头！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“在他们不再动弹之前，我决不休息！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“只要他们死，我们就能活！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“未来是血淋淋的！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“嗜血的未来是我们的！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“肢解生者，肢解死者！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我们要在成堆的尸体上庆祝！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“@player_name@，今天你将[享用|饱餐]极好的@_corpses_@！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“专攻要害！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我杀戮的冲动比他们的求生欲更强烈！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我浑身是血！感觉太棒了！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“啊哈！有块@_body_part_@粘我身上了。快擦掉它！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我的剑刃变钝了！我的剑刃变钝了！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“别把我塞到那个东西里面，拜托。”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“我连战皆捷！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“血一望无际！血生生世世！”","@The_weapon@@_screams_@：“鲜血必须喷涌，鲜血必须涌出！每个敌人都将血流如注！”"],"proposed_translation":["@The_weapon@喊道：“我在此以鲜血祝圣这些殿堂！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“情绪失控？胡说八道！我的钢铁永远坚韧！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“砍了他们！立刻砍掉他们所有的小脑袋！”","@The_weapon@叫道：“把他们都杀了，让{{
